How do I grow shrooms?=我如何种植蘑菇？
First, get a grain bag and a spore syringe. Take them both to a mushroom spawn station, where you can inject the spore syringe into the grain bag. This will produce shroom spawn.=首先，拿上一个谷物培养袋和一支孢子注射器。把它们带到蘑菇菌种培养台，将孢子注入谷物培养袋。这样就能培育出蘑菇菌种。
Once you've got your shroom spawn, take it to a mushroom bed that has been filled with mushroom substrate. Mix the spawn in to start the grow process.=拿到蘑菇菌种后，把它带到装有蘑菇基质的蘑菇床上，再将菌种拌入基质，开始培育。

I'd like to apologise for the minor altercation we had earlier involving your RV. We have a rather successful operation here in Hyland Point, and we're not keen on sharing the market.=早先为了你那辆房车起的那点小冲突，我道个歉。我们在海兰角生意做得不错，不太想跟人分市场。
Go ahead=说吧
However, I'd like to extend an offer. You will sell us product on an ongoing basis; 2-3 times per week. Large quantities and wholesale prices.=不过，我想跟你谈笔生意。以后你要定期给我们供货，每周两三次，量要大，按批发价算。
Additionally, you will refrain from dealing with customers outside of Northtown and Westville. The rest of Hyland Point is our territory - end of story.=另外，别跟北镇、西村以外的客人做生意。海兰角其他地方是我们的地盘，没得商量。
What's in it for me?=那我有什么好处？
In return, we will not blow up any more of your property, and will not harass you in any way. You're free to continue operating in your corner of the market, as long as you uphold your end of the deal.=作为回报，我们不会再炸你的房产，也不会再找你麻烦。只要你守好自己这边，你那一角市场可以继续做。
[REFUSE DEAL]=[拒绝交易]

How much product do you need?=你需要多少产品？
How much will you pay me?=你会付我多少钱？
Certainly not market prices - that's for sure. About half of what you'd usually get. We've got to make money somehow, don't we?=当然不是市场价——这是肯定的。价格大约只有你平常售价的一半。我们总得想办法赚钱，对吧？
Anything else?=还有别的吗？
10-15g a few times a week. Once we know you're reliable, the quantity will increase.=每周几次，每次 10–15 克。等我们确认你靠得住，订货量还会增加。
Maybe I wasn't clear - you WILL be working with us. And if you don't, we're going to make your life very, very difficult. Is that clear?=也许我没说清楚——你必须和我们合作。如果你不合作，我们会让你的日子非常、非常难过。明白了吗？
Fine, you've got a deal.=好吧，成交。
Crystal clear. Now piss off.=一清二楚。现在滚开。

And by the way, step out of line in any way, and you're done for. Take care...=顺便说一句，只要你敢越线，你就完蛋了。保重……
Drop off at Hyland Manor. Don't be late.=送到海兰庄园。别迟到。

You've hurt the Benzies pretty bad, but they ain't dealt with yet. They'll keep pestering you until they're wiped out.=你重创了本吉斯家族，但他们还没完蛋。他们会一直缠着你，直到被灭掉。
Thomas and the last of his goons are holed up in Hyland Manor. It's a well fortified old place, so attacking them head on is a death wish. Luckily, there might be another way...=托马斯和他最后的几个打手窝在海兰庄园里。那是个守得很严的老地方，正面攻击等于送死。不过，也许还有别的办法……
There's an old <color\=#4CB0FF>escape tunnel</color> that runs from the manor down to the cliff face beneath it. It's sealed up from the inside, but if you could get access to it, you could get real close to them without their knowing...=有一条旧的<color\=#4CB0FF>逃生隧道</color>，从庄园一直通到下面的悬崖。里面封死了，但你要是能进去，就能悄悄接近他们……
I say you treat these clowns to a taste of their own medicine. If you could plant a <color\=#4CB0FF>bomb</color> in that tunnel... well, safe to say they won't be a problem no more. =我看，让这帮小丑也尝尝他们自己的手段。要是你能在那条隧道里安放一颗<color\=#4CB0FF>炸弹</color>……他们以后就不再是麻烦了。
A bomb of that size won't be easy to come by - go ask around <color\=#4CB0FF>Koyama Chemical Company</color> and see if someone can sneak you some raw explosives. After that, <color\=#4CB0FF>Stan</color>, the weapons dealer, should be able to wire it up to a usable bomb for you.=那么大的炸弹可不好找——去<color\=#4CB0FF>小山化工公司</color>打听一下，看有没有人能偷偷给你一些炸药原料。之后，军火商<color\=#4CB0FF>斯坦</color>应该能帮你把它改装成能用的炸弹。

Reduce the Benzies' influence in a region by defeating ambushes, interrupting their deals, unlocking customers, and spraying graffiti. =通过挫败他们的伏击、中断他们的交易、解锁顾客并喷涂涂鸦，削弱本吉斯家族在该地区的影响力。
While you're truced with the Benzies family, you can only sell to customers in Northtown or Westville.=与本吉斯家族休战期间，你只能向北镇或西村的顾客卖货。
Don't leave items attended for too long in dead drops, otherwise they might be stolen...=不要让物品在藏货点放太久，否则可能会被偷走……

There's an old escape tunnel that runs from the manor down to the cliff face beneath it. It's sealed up from the inside, but if you could get access to it, you could get real close to them without their knowing...=有一条旧的逃生隧道从庄园通往下方的悬崖。隧道从里面封住了，但如果你能进去，就能悄悄接近他们……
You'll also need to get into that tunnel. Sam, the local construction and demolition guy can probably help you out for the right price.=你还得想办法进那条隧道。本地搞建筑和拆除的萨姆，价钱合适的话大概能帮上忙。
